LiveSoccer.com

Tampa Bay Super Cup

Tournament event store built as three Fastr CMS blocks around two native product widgets: hero and offer pop-up, boot name customizer, Zone feature pills, and interactive venue map.

Preview

Switch the screen width to test Phone, Tablet, and Desktop layouts.

Open in a new tab

The code

Copy the Fastr CMS paste code or download the HTML file.

Block 1 - Top (header, hero, marquee, 20% off pop-up)

tampa-bay-super-cup--1-top--fastr-paste.html
<!--
  Partner: Tampa Super Cup 2026, Top (header, nav, hero, marquee, 20% off pop-up)
  Tampa Bay Super Cup Official Store, Soccer.com. Fastr CMS component, no iframe. Paste the whole file into one Fastr HTML component.
  Page order on soccer.com/tampa-bay-super-cup: Top, DX jerseys widget, Middle, DX cleats carousel, Bottom.
  Reserved heights, Chromium, 21 Oct 2026, local stand-in fonts: Mobile 828 (at 390) · 640+ 1006 (at 768) · 1024+ 1200 (at 1440)

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.getElementById('tbsc-top');
  if (!root) return;
  var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  function safe(fn) { try { fn(); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('Super Cup store:', e); } }
  // Hero words animate in once on load. The two photo panels slide toward the centre together and pause while the hero is off screen.
  function initHero() {
    var hero = root.querySelector('[data-hero]');
    if (!hero) return;
    hero.classList.add('is-in');
    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
    var strips = [].slice.call(hero.querySelectorAll('.nh-hero__strip'));
    new IntersectionObserver(function (es) {
      strips.forEach(function (el) { el.style.animationPlayState = es[0].isIntersecting ? 'running' : 'paused'; });
    }, { threshold: 0 }).observe(hero);
  }

  /* Osmo Supply · CSS Marquee · JavaScript verbatim from marquee-init.js except numbered deviations */
  function initCSSMarquee() {
    const pixelsPerSecond = 75;
    /* ⚠️DEVIATION M3: queries run from the page wrapper, not document. */
    const marquees = root.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee]');

    marquees.forEach(marquee => {
      marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
        const duplicate = list.cloneNode(true);
        marquee.appendChild(duplicate);
      });
    });

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      entries.forEach(entry => {
        entry.target.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list =>
          list.style.animationPlayState = entry.isIntersecting ? 'running' : 'paused'
        );
      });
    }, { threshold: 0 });

    /* ⚠️DEVIATION M4: re-measure duration after fonts/window load and via ResizeObserver so iOS Safari never locks a pre-layout width; scale speed to 35px/s below 768px. */
    function measureAll() {
      const speed = window.innerWidth < 768 ? 35 : pixelsPerSecond;
      marquees.forEach(marquee => {
        marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
          const w = Math.max(list.scrollWidth || 0, list.offsetWidth || 0);
          if (w > 0) {
            list.style.animationDuration = Math.max(12, w / speed) + 's';
          }
        });
      });
    }

    const ro = 'ResizeObserver' in window ? new ResizeObserver(() => measureAll()) : null;

    marquees.forEach(marquee => {
      marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
        list.style.animationDuration = (list.offsetWidth / pixelsPerSecond) + 's';
        list.style.animationPlayState = 'paused';
        if (ro) ro.observe(list);
      });
      observer.observe(marquee);
    });

    measureAll();
    window.addEventListener('load', measureAll, { once: true });
    if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
      document.fonts.ready.then(measureAll).catch(function () {});
    }
  }


  // 20% off adidas cleats pop-up: opened from the nav and the customiser offer box (the box sits in another component on the live page).
  function initPop() {
    var pop = root.querySelector('#tbsc-cleats-pop');
    if (!pop) return;
    var panel = pop.querySelector('.nh-pop__panel');
    var last = null;
    function open(from) {
      last = from || document.activeElement;
      pop.hidden = false;
      panel.focus();
      document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    }
    function close(restore) {
      pop.hidden = true;
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      if (restore !== false && last && last.focus) last.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
    function onKey(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { close(); return; }
      if (e.key !== 'Tab') return;
      var f = [].slice.call(panel.querySelectorAll('a[href],button'));
      if (!f.length) return;
      var first = f[0], end = f[f.length - 1];
      if (e.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || document.activeElement === panel)) { e.preventDefault(); end.focus(); }
      else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === end) { e.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
    }
    [].forEach.call(pop.querySelectorAll('[data-pop-close]'), function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { close(b.tagName !== 'A'); }); });
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-cleats-open]');
      if (!t) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      open(t);
    });
  }
  function initNavFallback() {
    function offsetPx() { return window.innerWidth < 768 ? '64px' : '146px'; }
    function syncTargets() {
      var carousels = document.querySelectorAll('.playerpass-carousel__outer-wrapper');
      if (carousels.length >= 2) {
        [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-apparel-nav]'), function (el) { el.hidden = false; });
      }
      if (!document.getElementById('tbsc-mid') && carousels.length > 0) {
        var cleatsCarousel = carousels[carousels.length - 1];
        if (!cleatsCarousel.id) cleatsCarousel.id = 'tbsc-mid';
      }
      var mid = document.getElementById('tbsc-mid');
      if (mid) mid.style.scrollMarginTop = offsetPx();
    }
    syncTargets();
    window.addEventListener('load', syncTargets, { once: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncTargets, { passive: true });
    root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href^="#"]');
      if (!a) return;
      var hash = a.getAttribute('href');
      if (!hash || hash.length < 2) return;
      syncTargets();
      var id = hash.slice(1);
      var target = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!target && id === 'tbsc-mid') {
        target = document.querySelector('.playerpass-carousel__outer-wrapper') || document.getElementById('nh-shoe') || document.getElementById('tbsc-bottom');
      }
      if (target) {
        target.style.scrollMarginTop = offsetPx();
        e.preventDefault();
        target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reducedMotion ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' });
        if (window.history && history.replaceState) {
          try { history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + location.search + hash); } catch (err) {}
        }
      }
    });
  }
  safe(initHero);
  if (!reducedMotion && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) safe(initCSSMarquee);
  safe(initPop);
  safe(initNavFallback);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.getElementById('tbsc-mid');
  if (!root) return;
  var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  function safe(fn) { try { fn(); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('Super Cup store:', e); } }
  /* Osmo Supply · CSS Marquee · JavaScript verbatim from marquee-init.js except numbered deviations */
  function initCSSMarquee() {
    const pixelsPerSecond = 75;
    /* ⚠️DEVIATION M3: queries run from the page wrapper, not document. */
    const marquees = root.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee]');

    marquees.forEach(marquee => {
      marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
        const duplicate = list.cloneNode(true);
        marquee.appendChild(duplicate);
      });
    });

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      entries.forEach(entry => {
        entry.target.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list =>
          list.style.animationPlayState = entry.isIntersecting ? 'running' : 'paused'
        );
      });
    }, { threshold: 0 });

    /* ⚠️DEVIATION M4: re-measure duration after fonts/window load and via ResizeObserver so iOS Safari never locks a pre-layout width; scale speed to 35px/s below 768px. */
    function measureAll() {
      const speed = window.innerWidth < 768 ? 35 : pixelsPerSecond;
      marquees.forEach(marquee => {
        marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
          const w = Math.max(list.scrollWidth || 0, list.offsetWidth || 0);
          if (w > 0) {
            list.style.animationDuration = Math.max(12, w / speed) + 's';
          }
        });
      });
    }

    const ro = 'ResizeObserver' in window ? new ResizeObserver(() => measureAll()) : null;

    marquees.forEach(marquee => {
      marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
        list.style.animationDuration = (list.offsetWidth / pixelsPerSecond) + 's';
        list.style.animationPlayState = 'paused';
        if (ro) ro.observe(list);
      });
      observer.observe(marquee);
    });

    measureAll();
    window.addEventListener('load', measureAll, { once: true });
    if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
      document.fonts.ready.then(measureAll).catch(function () {});
    }
  }
  if (!reducedMotion && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) safe(initCSSMarquee);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.getElementById('tbsc-bottom');
  if (!root) return;
  var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  function safe(fn) { try { fn(); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('Super Cup store:', e); } }
  /* Osmo Supply · CSS Marquee · JavaScript verbatim from marquee-init.js except numbered deviations */
  function initCSSMarquee() {
    const pixelsPerSecond = 75;
    /* ⚠️DEVIATION M3: queries run from the page wrapper, not document. */
    const marquees = root.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee]');

    marquees.forEach(marquee => {
      marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
        const duplicate = list.cloneNode(true);
        marquee.appendChild(duplicate);
      });
    });

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      entries.forEach(entry => {
        entry.target.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list =>
          list.style.animationPlayState = entry.isIntersecting ? 'running' : 'paused'
        );
      });
    }, { threshold: 0 });

    /* ⚠️DEVIATION M4: re-measure duration after fonts/window load and via ResizeObserver so iOS Safari never locks a pre-layout width; scale speed to 35px/s below 768px. */
    function measureAll() {
      const speed = window.innerWidth < 768 ? 35 : pixelsPerSecond;
      marquees.forEach(marquee => {
        marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
          const w = Math.max(list.scrollWidth || 0, list.offsetWidth || 0);
          if (w > 0) {
            list.style.animationDuration = Math.max(12, w / speed) + 's';
          }
        });
      });
    }

    const ro = 'ResizeObserver' in window ? new ResizeObserver(() => measureAll()) : null;

    marquees.forEach(marquee => {
      marquee.querySelectorAll('[data-css-marquee-list]').forEach(list => {
        list.style.animationDuration = (list.offsetWidth / pixelsPerSecond) + 's';
        list.style.animationPlayState = 'paused';
        if (ro) ro.observe(list);
      });
      observer.observe(marquee);
    });

    measureAll();
    window.addEventListener('load', measureAll, { once: true });
    if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
      document.fonts.ready.then(measureAll).catch(function () {});
    }
  }

  // Name on the boot (NCFC template): the image handler renders the real print, same service as the Soccer.com cleat customiser.
  // l1lc print location, l1f font, l1s size, l1c colour hex, l1v name.
  function initNameShoe() {
    var stage = root.querySelector('[data-name-shoe]');
    var form = root.querySelector('[data-name-shoe-form]');
    if (!stage || !form) return;
    var img = stage.querySelector('[data-name-shoe-img]');
    var input = form.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
    var status = form.querySelector('[data-name-shoe-status]');
    var BASE = 'https://www.imagehandler.net/preview/?istyle=0000&fmt=png&w=1000&h=1000&cmp=100&c=999&img=A1133371000&iset=0100&iindex=0095&branding=no&sty=STY_WEB&l1lc=LIH102&l1s=0.375&l1f=BLKNA';
    var timer = null, last = '', seq = 0;
    function clean(v) { return (v || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9 .'\-]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 5); }
    function colour() { var c = form.querySelector('input[name="colour"]:checked'); return c ? c.value : 'FFFFFF'; }
    function colourName() { var c = form.querySelector('input[name="colour"]:checked'); return c ? c.parentNode.textContent.trim() : 'White'; }
    function render(announce) {
      var raw = clean(input.value).trim();
      var name = raw || 'MASON';
      var url = BASE + '&l1c=' + colour() + '&l1v=' + encodeURIComponent(name);
      if (url !== last) {
        last = url;
        var reqId = ++seq;
        stage.classList.add('is-loading');
        var pre = new Image();
        pre.onload = function () { if (reqId === seq) { img.src = url; stage.classList.remove('is-loading'); } };
        pre.onerror = function () { if (reqId === seq) stage.classList.remove('is-loading'); };
        pre.src = url;
        img.alt = 'Black adidas Predator boot with a mint tongue and the name ' + name + ' printed on the side in ' + colourName().toLowerCase();
      }
      if (announce && raw) status.textContent = 'The boot preview now shows ' + name + ' in ' + colourName().toLowerCase() + '.';
    }
    input.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
      if (!e || !e.isComposing) {
        var stripped = (input.value || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9 .'\-]/g, '').slice(0, 5);
        if (input.value !== stripped) input.value = stripped;
      }
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(function () { render(false); }, 220);
    });
    input.addEventListener('change', function () { clearTimeout(timer); render(true); });
    input.addEventListener('blur', function () {
      var c = clean(input.value);
      if (input.value !== c) input.value = c;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      render(false);
    });
    form.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (e.target.name === 'colour') render(true); });
    form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); clearTimeout(timer); input.blur(); render(true); });
    form.addEventListener('focusin', function () { form.classList.add('is-open'); });
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { if (!form.contains(e.target)) form.classList.remove('is-open'); });
  }

  // Add to my home screen: the share sheet where the browser offers one, plus the short instruction note.
  function initAddHome() {
    var btn = root.querySelector('[data-add-home]');
    if (!btn) return;
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var note = root.querySelector('[data-add-home-note]');
      if (note) note.hidden = false;
      if (navigator.share) { navigator.share({ title: document.title || 'Tampa Bay Super Cup Official Store', url: location.href }).catch(function () {}); }
    });
  }

  // Weekend chips + mobile/tablet venue scroll buttons: show the cards for one weekend, or the event store alone, and refit the map.
  function initWeekFilter(onChange) {
    var chips = root.querySelector('[data-week-chips]');
    var list = root.querySelector('[data-locator-list]');
    var prevBtn = root.querySelector('[data-venues-prev]');
    var nextBtn = root.querySelector('[data-venues-next]');
    if (list && prevBtn && nextBtn) {
      prevBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { list.scrollBy({ left: -280, behavior: reducedMotion ? 'auto' : 'smooth' }); });
      nextBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { list.scrollBy({ left: 280, behavior: reducedMotion ? 'auto' : 'smooth' }); });
    }
    if (/iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '')) {
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('.store-locator__item-link, .nh-zone__cta'), function (a) {
        var m = (a.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/destination=([^&]+)/);
        if (m && m[1]) a.setAttribute('href', 'https://maps.apple.com/?daddr=' + m[1]);
      });
    }
    if (!chips) return;
    chips.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var btn = e.target.closest('[data-week]'); if (!btn) return;
      var week = btn.getAttribute('data-week');
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(chips.querySelectorAll('[data-week]'), function (c) { c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', c === btn ? 'true' : 'false'); });
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-week-head]'), function (h) { h.hidden = h.getAttribute('data-week-head') !== week; });
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-locator-item]'), function (item) {
        item.hidden = item.getAttribute('data-week').split(' ').indexOf(week) < 0;
      });
      if (list) list.scrollLeft = 0;
      if (onChange) onChange(week);
    });
  }

  /* Osmo Supply · Store Locator (Mapbox) · JavaScript verbatim except numbered deviations */
  function initStoreLocatorMapbox() {
    var cfg = {
      mapboxToken: ["pk","eyJ1Ijoic2FjaGF3ZHNlaSIsImEiOiJjbHY0YWUybDkwNzA2MnFtd2F4ZzVpeHR0In0","NNvZ8t7euKzaAGKpd6M7dw"].join("."),
      mapStyle: "mapbox://styles/sachawdsei/cmv7i4bfb008w01sm0zacavjm",
      /* ⚠️DEVIATION 7: Osmo's own Studio style replaced with the Super Cup "Faded" style (Mapbox Standard, faded theme, white roads), made in Mapbox Studio on the same account as the token. */
      projection: "mercator",
      fallbackCenter: [-82.45, 28.0],
      fallbackZoom: 9,
      /* ⚠️DEVIATION 8: fallback view moved from the UK to Tampa Bay. */
      selectedZoom: 12,
      storeZoom: 17,
      /* ⚠️DEVIATION 12: the event store zooms in to the building, not the town. */
      flyToDuration: 1200,
      fitPadding: { top: 96, bottom: 48, left: 64, right: 64 },
      fitMaxZoom: 13,
    };

    if (
      typeof mapboxgl === "undefined" ||
      !cfg.mapboxToken ||
      cfg.mapboxToken === "YOUR_MAPBOX_PUBLIC_TOKEN"
    ) {
      return;
    }

    mapboxgl.accessToken = cfg.mapboxToken;

    var wrapper = root.querySelector("[data-locator-init]");
    /* ⚠️DEVIATION 9: querySelector runs from the block wrapper, not document, so the block cannot catch another block's locator. */
    if (!wrapper || wrapper.dataset.locatorInit === "initialized") return;

    var mapEl = wrapper.querySelector("[data-locator-map]");
    if (!mapEl) return;

    var locations = readLocations(wrapper);

    var map = new mapboxgl.Map({
      container: mapEl,
      style: cfg.mapStyle,
      center: cfg.fallbackCenter,
      zoom: cfg.fallbackZoom,
      projection: cfg.projection,
      pitch: 0,
      bearing: 0,
      attributionControl: false,
      cooperativeGestures: true,
    });

    map.addControl(new mapboxgl.AttributionControl({ compact: true }));
    map.addControl(
      new mapboxgl.NavigationControl({ showCompass: false }),
      "top-left"
    );

    map.on("load", function () {
      var pins = addPins(map, locations, wrapper);

      locations.forEach(function (location, index) {
        location.element.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
          if (event.target.closest("a, button, input, select, textarea")) return;
          selectLocation(index);
        });

        location.element.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
          if (event.target !== location.element) return;
          if (event.key !== "Enter" && event.key !== " ") return;
          event.preventDefault();
          selectLocation(index);
        });
      });

      pins.forEach(function (pin, index) {
        pin.element.addEventListener("click", function () {
          selectLocation(index);
        });

        pin.element.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
          if (event.key !== "Enter" && event.key !== " ") return;
          event.preventDefault();
          selectLocation(index);
        });
      });

      function selectLocation(index) {
        var location = locations[index];
        if (!location) return;

        pins.forEach(function (pin, pinIndex) {
          var isActive = pinIndex === index;
          pin.element.setAttribute("data-active", String(isActive));
          pin.element.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isActive));
          locations[pinIndex].element.setAttribute(
            "data-active",
            String(isActive)
          );
        });

        var camera = {
          center: [location.lng, location.lat],
          zoom: location.element.hasAttribute("data-store") ? cfg.storeZoom : cfg.selectedZoom,
          pitch: 0,
          bearing: 0,
        };

        if (reducedMotion) {
          map.jumpTo(camera);
        } else {
          map.flyTo(Object.assign({}, camera, {
            duration: cfg.flyToDuration,
            essential: false,
          }));
        }
      }

      /* ⚠️DEVIATION 10: pins follow the weekend filter; hidden cards hide their pins and the map refits to what is left. The chips work on their own, so the list still filters if the map never loads. */
      function applyWeek() {
        var visible = [];
        pins.forEach(function (pin, index) {
          var hide = locations[index].element.hidden;
          pin.element.style.display = hide ? "none" : "";
          if (!hide) visible.push(locations[index]);
        });
        fitToLocations(map, visible, reducedMotion);
      }
      onWeek = applyWeek;
      applyWeek();
      wrapper.dataset.locatorInit = "initialized";
    });

    var resizeTimer;
    window.addEventListener(
      "resize",
      function () {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
        resizeTimer = setTimeout(function () { map.resize(); }, 150);
      },
      { passive: true }
    );

    function readLocations(wrapper) {
      return Array.prototype.slice.call(wrapper.querySelectorAll("[data-locator-item]"))
        .map(function (element, index) {
          var lat = Number.parseFloat(element.getAttribute("data-locator-lat"));
          var lng = Number.parseFloat(element.getAttribute("data-locator-lng"));

          return {
            id: element.getAttribute("data-locator-id") || ("location-" + (index + 1)),
            name:
              text(element, "[data-locator-item-name]") ||
              ("Location " + (index + 1)),
            lat: lat,
            lng: lng,
            element: element,
          };
        })
        .filter(function (location) {
          return Number.isFinite(location.lat) && Number.isFinite(location.lng);
        });
    }

    function addPins(map, locations, wrapper) {
      var template = wrapper.querySelector("[data-locator-marker-template]");

      return locations.map(function (location) {
        var element;

        if (template instanceof HTMLTemplateElement) {
          element = template.content.firstElementChild.cloneNode(true);
        } else if (template) {
          element = template.cloneNode(true);
          element.removeAttribute("data-locator-marker-template");
        } else {
          element = document.createElement("button");
          element.type = "button";
          element.className = "locator-marker";
          element.innerHTML =
            '<span class="locator-marker__dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>';
        }

        element.setAttribute("data-locator-marker", location.id);
        element.setAttribute("data-active", "false");
        element.setAttribute("aria-label", "Show " + location.name);
        element.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
        /* ⚠️DEVIATION 11: the event store card's pin is the Soccer.com shop pin, tip on the spot, above the venue pins. */
        if (location.element.hasAttribute("data-store")) {
          var logo = wrapper.querySelector("[data-locator-store-template]");
          if (logo) element.innerHTML = logo.innerHTML;
          element.classList.add("store-locator__marker--store");
          element.style.zIndex = "2";
        }

        if (!element.matches("button, a, input")) {
          element.setAttribute("role", "button");
          element.setAttribute("tabindex", "0");
        }

        var marker = new mapboxgl.Marker({
          element: element,
          /* ⚠️DEVIATION 13: custom map pins, anchored on their tip. */
          anchor: "bottom",
          offset: [0, 6],
        })
          .setLngLat([location.lng, location.lat])
          .addTo(map);

        return { marker: marker, element: element, location: location };
      });
    }

    function fitToLocations(map, locations, reducedMotion) {
      if (!locations.length) return;

      if (locations.length === 1) {
        map.jumpTo({
          center: [locations[0].lng, locations[0].lat],
          zoom: locations[0].element.hasAttribute("data-store") ? cfg.storeZoom : cfg.selectedZoom,
        });
        return;
      }

      var bounds = new mapboxgl.LngLatBounds();
      locations.forEach(function (location) { bounds.extend([location.lng, location.lat]); });

      map.fitBounds(bounds, {
        padding: cfg.fitPadding,
        maxZoom: cfg.fitMaxZoom,
        duration: reducedMotion ? 0 : 700,
      });
    }

    function text(parent, selector) {
      var element = parent.querySelector(selector);
      return element ? element.textContent.trim() : "";
    }
  }
  if (!reducedMotion && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) safe(initCSSMarquee);
  var mapStarted = false;
  var mapLoading = false;
  var pendingStoreSelect = false;
  function startMap() {
    if (mapStarted) return true;
    if (typeof mapboxgl === "undefined") return false;
    mapStarted = true;
    safe(initStoreLocatorMapbox);
    if (pendingStoreSelect) {
      pendingStoreSelect = false;
      var card = root.querySelector('#tbsc-store-card');
      if (card) setTimeout(function () { card.click(); }, 50);
    }
    return true;
  }
  function loadMap() {
    if (startMap() || mapLoading) return;
    mapLoading = true;
    var mount = root.querySelector('[data-locator-script]') || root;
    var s = document.createElement('script');
    s.src = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/mapbox-gl@3.20.0/dist/mapbox-gl.js';
    s.async = true;
    s.onload = function () { startMap(); };
    mount.appendChild(s);
  }
  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href="#tbsc-store-card"]');
    var card = root.querySelector('#tbsc-store-card');
    if (a && card) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.style.scrollMarginTop = window.innerWidth < 768 ? '64px' : '146px';
      card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reducedMotion ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' });
      if (window.history && history.replaceState) {
        try { history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + location.search + '#tbsc-store-card'); } catch (err) {}
      }
      pendingStoreSelect = true;
      loadMap();
      setTimeout(function () { card.click(); }, 0);
    }
  });
  /* Feature Pills engine (canon: Feature Pills / PUMA Ultra Nitro). ⚠️DEVIATION FP1: the open pill keeps its title row and grows downward, so the heading, the other pills above it and the logos never move. ⚠️DEVIATION FP2: the list reserves room for its tallest open state. ⚠️DEVIATION FP3: CSS easing replaces GSAP back.out(2), no CDN. */
  function initFeaturePills() {
    var wrap = root.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-init]');
    if (!wrap) return;
    var list = wrap.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-list]');
    var items = Array.prototype.slice.call(wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-feature-pills-item]'));
    var visuals = Array.prototype.slice.call(wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-feature-pills-visual]'));
    var cover = wrap.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-cover]');
    var closeBtn = wrap.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-close]');
    if (!list || !items.length) return;
    var active = null;

    function closedH(item) { return Math.ceil(item.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-button]').getBoundingClientRect().height); }
    function openH(item) { return closedH(item) + Math.ceil(item.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-content]').getBoundingClientRect().height); }
    function sizeItems() {
      var gap = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(list).rowGap) || 0;
      var total = gap * (items.length - 1);
      var extra = 0;
      items.forEach(function (item, i) {
        var c = closedH(item);
        total += c;
        extra = Math.max(extra, openH(item) - c);
        item.style.height = (i === active ? openH(item) : c) + 'px';
      });
      list.style.height = (total + extra) + 'px';
    }
    function render() {
      items.forEach(function (item, i) {
        var open = i === active;
        item.setAttribute('data-active', open ? 'true' : 'false');
        item.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-button]').setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
        item.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-content]').setAttribute('aria-hidden', open ? 'false' : 'true');
        item.style.height = (open ? openH(item) : closedH(item)) + 'px';
      });
      visuals.forEach(function (v, i) {
        var on = i === active;
        v.setAttribute('data-active', on ? 'true' : 'false');
        v.setAttribute('aria-hidden', on ? 'false' : 'true');
      });
      wrap.setAttribute('data-feature-pills-active', active === null ? 'false' : 'true');
      if (cover) cover.setAttribute('data-active', active === null ? 'true' : 'false');
      if (closeBtn) {
        closeBtn.setAttribute('aria-hidden', active === null ? 'true' : 'false');
        closeBtn.tabIndex = active === null ? -1 : 0;
      }
    }
    function switchTo(i) { active = active === i ? null : i; render(); }
    function closeAll() {
      if (active === null) return;
      var btn = items[active].querySelector('[data-feature-pills-button]');
      active = null;
      render();
      btn.focus();
    }

    items.forEach(function (item, i) {
      item.querySelector('[data-feature-pills-button]').addEventListener('click', function () { switchTo(i); });
    });
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeAll);
    wrap.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeAll(); });

    sizeItems();
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { wrap.setAttribute('data-fp-ready', ''); });
    if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(sizeItems);
    var t;
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(sizeItems, 150); }, { passive: true });
  }
  safe(initNameShoe);
  safe(initFeaturePills);
  safe(initAddHome);
  var onWeek = null;
  safe(function () { initWeekFilter(function (w) { if (onWeek) onWeek(w); }); });
  var locator = root.querySelector('.nh-locator') || root.querySelector('[data-locator-init]');
  if (locator && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) {
    var mapObs = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      if (entries[0] && entries[0].isIntersecting) {
        mapObs.disconnect();
        loadMap();
      }
    }, { rootMargin: '800px 0px' });
    mapObs.observe(locator);
  } else {
    loadMap();
  }
})();
</script>
